1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Additivity test always passes (telescoping sum) – need factorial design
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍﻿. Confidence intervals require block/stationary bootstrap (ordinary resampling breaks long-range correlation structure)
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Need placebo-annotation control
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. Need Arabic control text (not just English KJB)
5﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Series definition must be committed to, or all options run and reported
